# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

Adds a new item line to the order when the order is pending and the library item is available.
Prevents duplicate item lines for the same library item.
If the library item is a print-format book item or a disc digital item, its status is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD.

Algorithm:

   - If not, return false.
2. Check whether itemLine is valid and itemLine.libraryItem.status == AVAILABLE.
   - If not, return false.
3. Check whether an existing item line in order.itemLines has the same libraryItem as itemLine.libraryItem.
   - If yes, return false.
4. Add itemLine to order.itemLines.
5. If itemLine.libraryItem is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT:
     set libraryItem.status = HOLD.
   Else if itemLine.libraryItem is a DigitalItem with option DISC:
     set libraryItem.status = HOLD.
   Else:
     keep libraryItem.status = AVAILABLE.
6. Return true.

Removes an item line from a pending order.
If the order is completed, the operation fails and returns -1.
When removal succeeds, the library item's status is reset to AVAILABLE only if it was previously HOLD
and only for print-format book items or disc digital items.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status == COMPLETED, return -1.

   - Per requirement, treat as failure; return -1 to indicate removal failure.
5. Remove the matching item line from order.itemLines.
6. Let item = removedItemLine.libraryItem.
7. If item.status == HOLD and item is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT:
     set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   Else if item.status == HOLD and item is a DigitalItem with option DISC:
     set item.status = AVAILABLE.

Output:
- int
  - On success: the updated number of item lines remaining in the order.
  - On failure because the order is completed: -1.
  - On other removal failure cases (e.g., item line not found, invalid input): -1.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists.
- itemLine is not null.
- itemLine.libraryItem is not null.
- The order is expected to be in PENDING status for successful removal.
Post-condition:
- If the operation succeeds:
  - The matching item line is removed from order.itemLines.
  - The returned value equals the new item line count.
  - The removed item's status is set to AVAILABLE only if it was previously HOLD and the item is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem or DISC DigitalItem.
  - Otherwise, the removed item's status remains unchanged.
- If the operation fails:
  - order.itemLines remains unchanged.
  - Library item statuses remain unchanged.
  - Returns -1 when the order is COMPLETED or when removal cannot be performed.}

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml

    type : BookItemType
}

    status : LibraryItemStatus

    DISC

    VIDEO

    PENDING
    COMPLETED
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary:
Counts the total quantity of print-format book items in this order, but only when the order is completed.
If the order is not completed, the result is 0.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status != COMPLETED, return 0.
2. Initialize totalPrintBooks = 0.
3. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   a. If itemLine.libraryItem is a BookItem and itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
      - Add itemLine.quantity to totalPrintBooks.
4. Return totalPrintBooks.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- Order.countPrintBookItemIfCompleted()
- ItemLine.quantity
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- BookItem.type
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, BookItemType, OrderStatus
Input:
- None.
Output:
- int
  - The total quantity of print-format book items in the order.
  - Range: integer >= 0.
  - Returns 0 if order.status is not COMPLETED or if no print-format book item exists.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists.
- order.itemLines is well-formed.
Post-condition:
- No system state is modified.
- The return value equals the sum of quantities of all item lines whose library item is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem, if and only if the order is COMPLETED.
- Otherwise, the return value is 0.

Order::handleOrder
Summary:
Finalizes the order by marking it as completed and converting eligible held items to loan status.
Only items that are print-format book items or disc digital items are transitioned from HOLD to LOAN.
Algorithm:
1. Set order.status = COMPLETED.
2. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   b. If item.status == HOLD:
      i. If item is a BookItem and item.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
         set item.status = LOAN.
      ii. Else if item is a DigitalItem and item.option == DISC:
         set item.status = LOAN.
      iii. Otherwise, keep item.status unchanged.
3. Return void.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- Order.handleOrder()
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- LibraryItem.status
- BookItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- None.
Output:
- void
  - No explicit return value.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists.
- order.itemLines is well-formed.
- For correct behavior, the order is expected to be in PENDING status before invocation.
Post-condition:
- order.status is set to COMPLETED.
- Each eligible item line's library item changes from HOLD to LOAN only when:
  - it is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem, or
  - it is a DISC DigitalItem.
- All other library items retain their current status.
- The order remains completed after the call.

Order::addItemLine
Summary:
Adds a new item line to the order when the order is pending and the library item is available.
Prevents duplicate item lines for the same library item.
If the library item is a print-format book item or a disc digital item, its status is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD.
Otherwise, the library item remains AVAILABLE.
Algorithm:
1. Check whether order.status == PENDING.
   - If not, return false.
2. Check whether itemLine is valid and itemLine.libraryItem.status == AVAILABLE.
   - If not, return false.
3. Check whether an existing item line in order.itemLines has the same libraryItem as itemLine.libraryItem.
   - If yes, return false.
4. Add itemLine to order.itemLines.
5. If itemLine.libraryItem is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT:
     set libraryItem.status = HOLD.
   Else if itemLine.libraryItem is a DigitalItem with option DISC:
     set libraryItem.status = HOLD.
   Else:
     keep libraryItem.status = AVAILABLE.
6. Return true.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- Order.addItemLine(itemLine)
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- ItemLine.quantity
- LibraryItem.status
- BookItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- itemLine: ItemLine
  - The item line to be inserted into the order.
  - itemLine.quantity must be a positive integer.
  - itemLine.libraryItem must not be null.
  - The library item must currently be AVAILABLE.
Output:
- boolean
  - true if the item line is successfully added.
  - false if validation fails, including:
    - order is not PENDING,
    - library item is not AVAILABLE,
    - duplicate library item already exists in the order,
    - itemLine is invalid.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists and is in PENDING status.
- itemLine is not null.
- itemLine.libraryItem is not null.
- itemLine.quantity > 0.
- The library item referenced by itemLine is AVAILABLE before the call.
- The order does not already contain an item line for the same library item.
Post-condition:
- If the operation succeeds:
  - itemLine is appended to order.itemLines.
  - The order still remains in PENDING status.
  - The referenced library item status becomes HOLD only when the item is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem or a DISC DigitalItem.
  - Otherwise, the referenced library item remains AVAILABLE.
- If the operation fails:
  - order.itemLines is unchanged.
  - Library item statuses are unchanged.
- The returned value reflects success or failure.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary:
Removes an item line from a pending order.
If the order is completed, the operation fails and returns -1.
When removal succeeds, the library item's status is reset to AVAILABLE only if it was previously HOLD
and only for print-format book items or disc digital items.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status == COMPLETED, return -1.
2. If order.status != PENDING, return -1.
3. Search order.itemLines for an item line matching the given itemLine.
   - Matching is based on the same library item.
4. If no matching item line exists, return the current number of item lines unchanged? 
   - Per requirement, treat as failure; return -1 to indicate removal failure.
5. Remove the matching item line from order.itemLines.
6. Let item = removedItemLine.libraryItem.
7. If item.status == HOLD and item is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT:
     set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   Else if item.status == HOLD and item is a DigitalItem with option DISC:
     set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   Otherwise:
     keep item.status unchanged.
8. Return the updated count of item lines in the order.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- Order.removeItemLine(itemLine)
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- LibraryItem.status
- BookItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- itemLine: ItemLine
  - The item line to remove.
  - itemLine.libraryItem must identify the target library item to remove.
Output:
- int
  - On success: the updated number of item lines remaining in the order.
  - On failure because the order is completed: -1.
  - On other removal failure cases (e.g., item line not found, invalid input): -1.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists.
- itemLine is not null.
- itemLine.libraryItem is not null.
- The order is expected to be in PENDING status for successful removal.
Post-condition:
- If the operation succeeds:
  - The matching item line is removed from order.itemLines.
  - The returned value equals the new item line count.
  - The removed item's status is set to AVAILABLE only if it was previously HOLD and the item is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem or DISC DigitalItem.
  - Otherwise, the removed item's status remains unchanged.
- If the operation fails:
  - order.itemLines remains unchanged.
  - Library item statuses remain unchanged.
  - Returns -1 when the order is COMPLETED or when removal cannot be performed.

```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary:
Calculates the total number of downloads for this Audio digital item across a list of orders.
Only completed orders are considered, and only item lines containing this same digital item
contribute to the result. If this digital item is not downloadable, the result is 0.
Algorithm:
1. If this DigitalItem.option is DISC, return 0 immediately.
2. If this DigitalItem.type is not AUDIO, return 0.
3. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
4. For each order in orders:
   a. Consider the order only if order.status == COMPLETED.
   b. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
      i. If itemLine.libraryItem refers to this DigitalItem instance:
         - Add itemLine.quantity to totalDownloads.
5. Return totalDownloads.
Related Features:
- DigitalItem.option
- DigitalItem.type
- DigitalItem.calculateTotalDownloads(orders)
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.quantity
- ItemLine.libraryItem
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, DigitalItemOption, DigitalItemType, OrderStatus, LibraryItem
Input:
- orders: List<Order>
  - A list of orders to inspect.
  - May be empty, in which case the result is 0.
  - Only orders with status COMPLETED are counted.
Output:
- int
  - The total number of downloads for this audio digital item.
  - Range: integer >= 0.
  - Returns 0 if this item is not downloadable, if it is not an AUDIO item, or if no matching completed order exists.
Pre-condition:
- orders is not null.
- This object is a valid DigitalItem instance.
- The item is intended to be used as an Audio digital item for this operation.
- Each order and item line in orders is well-formed.
Post-condition:
- No system state is modified.
- The returned value equals the sum of quantities of matching item lines in completed orders for this downloadable audio item.
- If no matching completed order/item line exists, the result is 0.

   * Adds a new item line to the order when the order is pending and the library item is available.
   * Prevents duplicate item lines for the same library item.
   * If the library item is a print-format book item or a disc digital item, its status is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD.
   * Otherwise, the library item remains AVAILABLE.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Check whether order.status == PENDING.
   *    - If not, return false.
   * 2. Check whether itemLine is valid and itemLine.libraryItem.status == AVAILABLE.
   *    - If not, return false.
   * 3. Check whether an existing item line in order.itemLines has the same libraryItem as itemLine.libraryItem.
   *    - If yes, return false.
   * 4. Add itemLine to order.itemLines.
   * 5. If itemLine.libraryItem is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT:
   *      set libraryItem.status = HOLD.
   *    Else if itemLine.libraryItem is a DigitalItem with option DISC:
   *      set libraryItem.status = HOLD.
   *    Else:
   *      keep libraryItem.status = AVAILABLE.
   * 6. Return true.
   * Related Features:
   * - Order.status
   * - Order.itemLines
   * - Order.addItemLine(itemLine)
   * - ItemLine.libraryItem
   * - ItemLine.quantity
   * - LibraryItem.status
   * - BookItem.type
   * - DigitalItem.option
   * Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
   * Input:
   * - itemLine: ItemLine
   *   - The item line to be inserted into the order.
   *   - itemLine.quantity must be a positive integer.
   *   - itemLine.libraryItem must not be null.
   *   - The library item must currently be AVAILABLE.
   * Output:
   * - boolean
   *   - true if the item line is successfully added.
   *   - false if validation fails, including:
   *     - order is not PENDING,
   *     - library item is not AVAILABLE,
   *     - duplicate library item already exists in the order,
   *     - itemLine is invalid.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - The order exists and is in PENDING status.
   * - itemLine is not null.
   * - itemLine.libraryItem is not null.
   * - itemLine.quantity > 0.
   * - The library item referenced by itemLine is AVAILABLE before the call.
   * - The order does not already contain an item line for the same library item.
   * Post-condition:
   * - If the operation succeeds:
   *   - itemLine is appended to order.itemLines.
   *   - The order still remains in PENDING status.
   *   - The referenced library item status becomes HOLD only when the item is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem or a DISC DigitalItem.
   *   - Otherwise, the referenced library item remains AVAILABLE.
   * - If the operation fails:
   *   - order.itemLines is unchanged.
   *   - Library item statuses are unchanged.
   * - The returned value reflects success or failure.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model itemLineRequired="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING || itemLine == null || itemLine.getLibraryItem() == null || itemLine.getQuantity() <= 0)
    {
      return false;
    }

    LibraryItem libraryItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
    if (libraryItem.getStatus() != LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE)
    {
      return false;
    }

    for (ItemLine existingItemLine : getItemLines())
    {
      if (existingItemLine.getLibraryItem() == libraryItem)
      {
        return false;
      }
    }

    getItemLines().add(itemLine);

    if (libraryItem instanceof BookItem)
    {
      BookItem bookItem = (BookItem)libraryItem;
      if (bookItem.getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT)
      {
        libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
      }
    }
    else if (libraryItem instanceof DigitalItem)
    {
      DigitalItem digitalItem = (DigitalItem)libraryItem;
      if (digitalItem.getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC)
      {
        libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
      }
    }

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Counts the total quantity of print-format book items in this order, but only when the order is completed.
   * If the order is not completed, the result is 0.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. If order.status != COMPLETED, return 0.
   * 2. Initialize totalPrintBooks = 0.
   * 3. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   *    a. If itemLine.libraryItem is a BookItem and itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
   *       - Add itemLine.quantity to totalPrintBooks.
   * 4. Return totalPrintBooks.
   * Related Features:
   * - Order.status
   * - Order.itemLines
   * - Order.countPrintBookItemIfCompleted()
   * - ItemLine.quantity
   * - ItemLine.libraryItem
   * - BookItem.type
   * Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, BookItemType, OrderStatus
   * Input:
   * - None.
   * Output:
   * - int
   *   - The total quantity of print-format book items in the order.
   *   - Range: integer >= 0.
   *   - Returns 0 if order.status is not COMPLETED or if no print-format book item exists.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - The order exists.
   * - order.itemLines is well-formed.
   * Post-condition:
   * - No system state is modified.
   * - The return value equals the sum of quantities of all item lines whose library item is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem, if and only if the order is COMPLETED.
   * - Otherwise, the return value is 0.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int countPrintBookItemIfCompleted()
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.COMPLETED)
    {
      return 0;
    }

    int totalPrintBooks = 0;
    for (ItemLine itemLine : getItemLines())
    {
      LibraryItem libraryItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
      if (libraryItem instanceof BookItem && ((BookItem)libraryItem).getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT)
      {
        totalPrintBooks += itemLine.getQuantity();
      }
    }
    return totalPrintBooks;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Removes an item line from a pending order.
   * If the order is completed, the operation fails and returns -1.
   * When removal succeeds, the library item's status is reset to AVAILABLE only if it was previously HOLD
   * and only for print-format book items or disc digital items.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. If order.status == COMPLETED, return -1.
   * 2. If order.status != PENDING, return -1.
   * 3. Search order.itemLines for an item line matching the given itemLine.
   *    - Matching is based on the same library item.
   * 4. If no matching item line exists, return the current number of item lines unchanged? 
   *    - Per requirement, treat as failure; return -1 to indicate removal failure.
   * 5. Remove the matching item line from order.itemLines.
   * 6. Let item = removedItemLine.libraryItem.
   * 7. If item.status == HOLD and item is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT:
   *      set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   *    Else if item.status == HOLD and item is a DigitalItem with option DISC:
   *      set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   *    Otherwise:
   *      keep item.status unchanged.
   * 8. Return the updated count of item lines in the order.
   * Related Features:
   * - Order.status
   * - Order.itemLines
   * - Order.removeItemLine(itemLine)
   * - ItemLine.libraryItem
   * - LibraryItem.status
   * - BookItem.type
   * - DigitalItem.option
   * Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
   * Input:
   * - itemLine: ItemLine
   *   - The item line to remove.
   *   - itemLine.libraryItem must identify the target library item to remove.
   * Output:
   * - int
   *   - On success: the updated number of item lines remaining in the order.
   *   - On failure because the order is completed: -1.
   *   - On other removal failure cases (e.g., item line not found, invalid input): -1.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - The order exists.
   * - itemLine is not null.
   * - itemLine.libraryItem is not null.
   * - The order is expected to be in PENDING status for successful removal.
   * Post-condition:
   * - If the operation succeeds:
   *   - The matching item line is removed from order.itemLines.
   *   - The returned value equals the new item line count.
   *   - The removed item's status is set to AVAILABLE only if it was previously HOLD and the item is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem or DISC DigitalItem.
   *   - Otherwise, the removed item's status remains unchanged.
   * - If the operation fails:
   *   - order.itemLines remains unchanged.
   *   - Library item statuses remain unchanged.
   *   - Returns -1 when the order is COMPLETED or when removal cannot be performed.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model itemLineRequired="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int removeItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING || itemLine == null || itemLine.getLibraryItem() == null)
    {
      return -1;
    }

    ItemLine target = null;
    for (ItemLine existingItemLine : getItemLines())
    {
      if (existingItemLine.getLibraryItem() == itemLine.getLibraryItem())
      {
        target = existingItemLine;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (target == null)
    {
      return -1;
    }

    getItemLines().remove(target);

    LibraryItem libraryItem = target.getLibraryItem();
    if (libraryItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD)
    {
      if (libraryItem instanceof BookItem && ((BookItem)libraryItem).getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT)
      {
        libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
      }
      else if (libraryItem instanceof DigitalItem && ((DigitalItem)libraryItem).getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC)
      {
        libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
      }
    }

    return getItemLines().size();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Finalizes the order by marking it as completed and converting eligible held items to loan status.
   * Only items that are print-format book items or disc digital items are transitioned from HOLD to LOAN.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Set order.status = COMPLETED.
   * 2. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   *    a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   *    b. If item.status == HOLD:
   *       i. If item is a BookItem and item.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
   *          set item.status = LOAN.
   *       ii. Else if item is a DigitalItem and item.option == DISC:
   *          set item.status = LOAN.
   *       iii. Otherwise, keep item.status unchanged.
   * 3. Return void.
   * Related Features:
   * - Order.status
   * - Order.itemLines
   * - Order.handleOrder()
   * - ItemLine.libraryItem
   * - LibraryItem.status
   * - BookItem.type
   * - DigitalItem.option
   * Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
   * Input:
   * - None.
   * Output:
   * - void
   *   - No explicit return value.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - The order exists.
   * - order.itemLines is well-formed.
   * - For correct behavior, the order is expected to be in PENDING status before invocation.
   * Post-condition:
   * - order.status is set to COMPLETED.
   * - Each eligible item line's library item changes from HOLD to LOAN only when:
   *   - it is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem, or
   *   - it is a DISC DigitalItem.
   * - All other library items retain their current status.
   * - The order remains completed after the call.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public void handleOrder()
  {
    setStatus(OrderStatus.COMPLETED);

    for (ItemLine itemLine : getItemLines())
    {
      LibraryItem libraryItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
      if (libraryItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD)
      {
        if (libraryItem instanceof BookItem && ((BookItem)libraryItem).getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT)
        {
          libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.LOAN);
        }
        else if (libraryItem instanceof DigitalItem && ((DigitalItem)libraryItem).getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC)
        {
          libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.LOAN);
        }
      }
    }
  }

```java
   * Summary:
   * Calculates the total number of downloads for this Audio digital item across a list of orders.
   * Only completed orders are considered, and only item lines containing this same digital item
   * contribute to the result. If this digital item is not downloadable, the result is 0.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. If this DigitalItem.option is DISC, return 0 immediately.
   * 2. If this DigitalItem.type is not AUDIO, return 0.
   * 3. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
```

  public int calculateTotalDownloads(final EList<Order> orders)
  {
    if (getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC)
    {
      return 0;
    }
    if (getType() != DigitalItemType.AUDIO)
    {
      return 0;
    }

    int totalDownloads = 0;
    for (Order order : orders)
    {
      if (order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.COMPLETED)
      {
        for (ItemLine itemLine : order.getItemLines())
        {
          if (itemLine.getLibraryItem() == this)
          {
            totalDownloads += itemLine.getQuantity();
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return totalDownloads;

6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}

    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}

    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

5. Remove the matching line from order.itemLines.

   a. If item.status == HOLD, set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   b. Otherwise, keep item.status unchanged.

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary:
Count the total quantity of print-format book items in this order, but only when the order is COMPLETED.
If the order is not completed, return 0.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status != COMPLETED, return 0.
2. Initialize totalCount = 0.
3. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   a. If itemLine.libraryItem is a BookItem and itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT,
      add itemLine.quantity to totalCount.
4. Return totalCount.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.quantity
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- BookItem.type
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, BookItemType, OrderStatus
Input:
- None
  The operation uses the current order instance only.
Output:
- int
  The total quantity of print-format book items in the order.
  Returns 0 if the order is not completed or if no matching item lines exist.
Pre-condition:
- The order instance exists.
- The order contains a valid list of item lines.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The returned value equals the sum of quantities of all item lines whose libraryItem is a BookItem
  with type PRINT_FORMAT, but only when the order status is COMPLETED.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary:
Remove an item line from an existing order, but only when the order is in PENDING status.
If the order is COMPLETED, the operation fails and returns -1.
When successful, the removed library item's status is reset to AVAILABLE only if it was previously HOLD
and the item is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or DISC digital item; otherwise the item status is retained.
The returned value is the updated number of item lines in the order.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status == COMPLETED, return -1.
2. If itemLine is null, return -1.
3. Find the matching line in order.itemLines by libraryItem reference.
4. If no matching line exists, return -1.
5. Remove the matching line from order.itemLines.
6. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
7. If item is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT or a DigitalItem with option DISC:
   a. If item.status == HOLD, set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   b. Otherwise, keep item.status unchanged.
8. Return order.itemLines.size().
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- LibraryItem.status
- BookItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- itemLine: ItemLine
  The item line to be removed.
  The line is identified by its associated libraryItem.
Output:
- int
  The updated count of item lines in the order after removal.
  Returns -1 if removal is not allowed or the specified line does not exist.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists.
- The order must be in PENDING status for removal to proceed.
- itemLine is non-null.
- The order must contain an item line referencing the same libraryItem as itemLine.
Post-condition:
- If returned value is not -1:
  - the matched item line is removed from order.itemLines;
  - order.itemLines size is reduced by 1;
  - if the removed item was HOLD and is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or DISC digital item,
    its status becomes AVAILABLE;
  - otherwise the library item status remains unchanged.
- If returned value is -1:
  - no state changes are applied.

Order::addItemLine
Summary:
Add a new item line to the order if all business rules are satisfied.
The order must be in PENDING status, the library item must be AVAILABLE, and no duplicate
item line for the same library item may already exist in the order.
If the item is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or DISC digital item, the library item's status
is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD after the line is added.
Otherwise, the library item's status remains AVAILABLE.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status != PENDING, return false.
2. If itemLine is null or itemLine.libraryItem is null, return false.
3. If itemLine.libraryItem.status != AVAILABLE, return false.
4. Scan order.itemLines:
   a. If any existing line has the same libraryItem as itemLine.libraryItem, return false.
5. Add itemLine to order.itemLines.
6. If itemLine.libraryItem is:
   a. BookItem with type = PRINT_FORMAT, or
   b. DigitalItem with option = DISC
   then set itemLine.libraryItem.status = HOLD.
   Otherwise, keep status as AVAILABLE.
7. Return true.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- LibraryItem.status
- BookItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- itemLine: ItemLine
  The item line to be added.
  itemLine.quantity must be a positive integer (> 0).
  itemLine.libraryItem identifies the library item to be ordered.
Output:
- boolean
  true if the item line is successfully added and any required status update is applied;
  false if validation fails.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists and is in PENDING status.
- The item line is non-null.
- itemLine.quantity > 0.
- itemLine.libraryItem is non-null.
- itemLine.libraryItem.status == AVAILABLE.
- No existing item line in this order references the same library item.
Post-condition:
- If returned true:
  - itemLine is appended to order.itemLines.
  - The order still remains in PENDING status.
  - The library item status becomes HOLD only for PRINT_FORMAT book items and DISC digital items;
    otherwise it remains AVAILABLE.
- If returned false:
  - order.itemLines is unchanged.
  - library item status is unchanged.

Order::handleOrder
Summary:
Complete the order and update the status of affected library items.
First, the order status is changed to COMPLETED.
Then, any library item in the order that is currently HOLD is changed to LOAN,
but only if the library item is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or a DISC digital item.
Other library items retain their current status.
Algorithm:
1. Set order.status = COMPLETED.
2. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   b. If item is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT and item.status == HOLD, set item.status = LOAN.
   c. Else if item is a DigitalItem with option = DISC and item.status == HOLD, set item.status = LOAN.
   d. Otherwise, do nothing.
3. Return void.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- LibraryItem.status
- BookItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- None
  The operation uses the current order instance only.
Output:
- void
  No direct return value.
Pre-condition:
- The order instance exists.
- The order contains valid item lines.
Post-condition:
- order.status is COMPLETED.
- For each item line:
  - if the referenced item is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or DISC digital item and its status was HOLD,
    then its status becomes LOAN;
  - otherwise its status remains unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary:
Calculate the total number of downloads for the current Audio digital item across a provided list of orders.
Only orders with status COMPLETED are considered, and only when this DigitalItem is DOWNLOADABLE.
The count is based on the quantity of matching item lines that reference this digital item.
Algorithm:
1. If this digital item is not of type AUDIO, return 0.
2. If this digital item option is not DOWNLOADABLE, return 0.
3. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
4. For each order in orders:
   a. If order.status != COMPLETED, skip it.
   b. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
      i. If itemLine.libraryItem == this and this item is a DigitalItem of type AUDIO and option DOWNLOADABLE,
         add itemLine.quantity to totalDownloads.
5. Return totalDownloads.
Related Features:
- DigitalItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.quantity
- ItemLine.libraryItem
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, DigitalItemType, DigitalItemOption, OrderStatus, LibraryItem
Input:
- orders: List<Order>
  A collection of orders to be scanned.
  May be empty, but should not be null.
Output:
- int
  The total quantity of downloads for this Audio digital item in completed orders.
  Returns 0 if no matching downloads exist.
Pre-condition:
- This object must represent a DigitalItem with:
  - type = AUDIO
  - option = DOWNLOADABLE
- orders must be a valid list of Order objects.
- Only completed orders are meaningful for the calculation.
Post-condition:
- No model state is modified.
- The returned value equals the sum of quantities of all item lines in completed orders
  whose libraryItem refers to this digital item.

```text
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary:
Calculate the total number of downloads for the current Audio digital item across a provided list of orders.
Only orders with status COMPLETED are considered, and only when this DigitalItem is DOWNLOADABLE.
The count is based on the quantity of matching item lines that reference this digital item.
Algorithm:
1. If this digital item is not of type AUDIO, return 0.
2. If this digital item option is not DOWNLOADABLE, return 0.
3. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
4. For each order in orders:
   a. If order.status != COMPLETED, skip it.
   b. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
      i. If itemLine.libraryItem == this and this item is a DigitalItem of type AUDIO and option DOWNLOADABLE,
         add itemLine.quantity to totalDownloads.
5. Return totalDownloads.
Related Features:
- DigitalItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.quantity
- ItemLine.libraryItem
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, DigitalItemType, DigitalItemOption, OrderStatus, LibraryItem
Input:
- orders: List<Order>
  A collection of orders to be scanned.
  May be empty, but should not be null.
Output:
- int
  The total quantity of downloads for this Audio digital item in completed orders.
  Returns 0 if no matching downloads exist.
Pre-condition:
- This object must represent a DigitalItem with:
  - type = AUDIO
  - option = DOWNLOADABLE
- orders must be a valid list of Order objects.
- Only completed orders are meaningful for the calculation.
Post-condition:
- No model state is modified.
- The returned value equals the sum of quantities of all item lines in completed orders
  whose libraryItem refers to this digital item.

```java
  /**

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Remove an item line from an existing order, but only when the order is in PENDING status.
   * If the order is COMPLETED, the operation fails and returns -1.
   * When successful, the removed library item's status is reset to AVAILABLE only if it was previously HOLD
   * and the item is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or DISC digital item; otherwise the item status is retained.
   * The returned value is the updated number of item lines in the order.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. If order.status == COMPLETED, return -1.
   * 2. If itemLine is null, return -1.
   * 3. Find the matching line in order.itemLines by libraryItem reference.
   * 4. If no matching line exists, return -1.
   * 5. Remove the matching line from order.itemLines.
   * 6. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   * 7. If item is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT or a DigitalItem with option DISC:
   *    a. If item.status == HOLD, set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   *    b. Otherwise, keep item.status unchanged.
   * 8. Return order.itemLines.size().
   * Related Features:

   * - Order.itemLines
   * - ItemLine.libraryItem

   * Output:
   * - int
   *   The updated count of item lines in the order after removal.
   *   Returns -1 if removal is not allowed or the specified line does not exist.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - The order exists.

   * - itemLine is non-null.
   * - The order must contain an item line referencing the same libraryItem as itemLine.
```

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Add a new item line to the order if all business rules are satisfied.
   * The order must be in PENDING status, the library item must be AVAILABLE, and no duplicate
   * item line for the same library item may already exist in the order.
   * If the item is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or DISC digital item, the library item's status
   * is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD after the line is added.
   * Otherwise, the library item's status remains AVAILABLE.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. If order.status != PENDING, return false.
   * 2. If itemLine is null or itemLine.libraryItem is null, return false.
   * 3. If itemLine.libraryItem.status != AVAILABLE, return false.
   * 4. Scan order.itemLines:
   *    a. If any existing line has the same libraryItem as itemLine.libraryItem, return false.
   * 5. Add itemLine to order.itemLines.
   * 6. If itemLine.libraryItem is:
   *    a. BookItem with type = PRINT_FORMAT, or
   *    b. DigitalItem with option = DISC
   *    then set itemLine.libraryItem.status = HOLD.
   *    Otherwise, keep status as AVAILABLE.
   * 7. Return true.

   * Input:
   * - itemLine: ItemLine
   *   The item line to be added.
   *   itemLine.quantity must be a positive integer (> 0).
   *   itemLine.libraryItem identifies the library item to be ordered.
   * Output:

   *   true if the item line is successfully added and any required status update is applied;
```

   *   - itemLine is appended to order.itemLines;
   *   - The order still remains in PENDING status;

   *     otherwise it remains AVAILABLE.
   * - If returned false:

   *   - library item status is unchanged.

  public boolean addItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (itemLine == null || itemLine.getLibraryItem() == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (itemLine.getLibraryItem().getStatus() != LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE)
    {
      return false;
    }
    for (ItemLine existingLine : getItemLines())
    {
      if (existingLine.getLibraryItem() == itemLine.getLibraryItem())
      {
        return false;
      }
    }
    getItemLines().add(itemLine);

    LibraryItem libraryItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
    if (libraryItem instanceof BookItem)
    {
      BookItem bookItem = (BookItem)libraryItem;
      if (bookItem.getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT)
      {
        libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
      }
    }
    else if (libraryItem instanceof DigitalItem)
    {
      DigitalItem digitalItem = (DigitalItem)libraryItem;
      if (digitalItem.getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC)
      {
        libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
      }
    }
    return true;
    
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Count the total quantity of print-format book items in this order, but only when the order is COMPLETED.
   * If the order is not completed, return 0.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. If order.status != COMPLETED, return 0.
   * 2. Initialize totalCount = 0.

   * Pre-condition:
   * - The order instance exists.
   * - The order contains a valid list of item lines.

  public int countPrintBookItemIfCompleted()
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.COMPLETED)
    {
      return 0;
    }

    int totalCount = 0;
    for (ItemLine itemLine : getItemLines())
    {
      LibraryItem libraryItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
      if (libraryItem instanceof BookItem)
      {
        BookItem bookItem = (BookItem)libraryItem;
        if (bookItem.getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT)
        {
          totalCount += itemLine.getQuantity();
        }
      }
    }
    return totalCount;
    
  }

   * - BookItem.type
   * - DigitalItem.option
   * Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
   * Input:
   * - itemLine: ItemLine
   *   The item line to be removed.
   *   The line is identified by its associated libraryItem.
   * Output:
   * - int
   *   The updated count of item lines in the order after removal.
   *   Returns -1 if removal is not allowed or the specified line does not exist.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - The order exists.
   * - The order must be in PENDING status for removal to proceed.
   * - itemLine is non-null.
   * - The order must contain an item line referencing the same libraryItem as itemLine.
   * Post-condition:
   * - If returned value is not -1:
   *   - the matched item line is removed from order.itemLines;
   *   - order.itemLines size is reduced by 1;
   *   - if the removed item was HOLD and is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or DISC digital item,
   *     its status becomes AVAILABLE;
   *   - otherwise the library item status remains unchanged.
   * - If returned value is -1:
   *   - no state changes are applied.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model itemLineRequired="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int removeItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
  {
    if (getStatus() == OrderStatus.COMPLETED || itemLine == null || itemLine.getLibraryItem() == null)
    {
      return -1;
    }

    ItemLine matchedLine = null;
    for (ItemLine existingLine : getItemLines())
    {
      if (existingLine.getLibraryItem() == itemLine.getLibraryItem())
      {
        matchedLine = existingLine;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (matchedLine == null)
    {
      return -1;
    }

    getItemLines().remove(matchedLine);

    LibraryItem libraryItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
    if (libraryItem instanceof BookItem)
    {
      BookItem bookItem = (BookItem)libraryItem;
      if (bookItem.getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT && libraryItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD)
      {
        libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
      }
    }
    else if (libraryItem instanceof DigitalItem)
    {
      DigitalItem digitalItem = (DigitalItem)libraryItem;
      if (digitalItem.getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC && libraryItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD)
      {
        libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
      }
    }
    return getItemLines().size();
    
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   * Complete the order and update the status of affected library items.
   * First, the order status is changed to COMPLETED.
   * Then, any library item in the order that is currently HOLD is changed to LOAN,
   * but only if the library item is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or a DISC digital item.
   * Other library items retain their current status.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. Set order.status = COMPLETED.
   * 2. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   *    a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   *    b. If item is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT and item.status == HOLD, set item.status = LOAN.
   *    c. Else if item is a DigitalItem with option = DISC and item.status == HOLD, set item.status = LOAN.
   *    d. Otherwise, do nothing.
   * 3. Return void.
   * Related Features:
   * - Order.status
   * - Order.itemLines
   * - ItemLine.libraryItem
   * - LibraryItem.status
   * - BookItem.type
   * - DigitalItem.option
   * Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
   * Input:
   * - None
   *   The operation uses the current order instance only.
   * Output:
   * - void
   *   No direct return value.
   * Pre-condition:
   * - The order instance exists.
   * - The order contains valid item lines.
   * Post-condition:
   * - order.status is COMPLETED.
   * - For each item line:
   *   - if the referenced item is a PRINT_FORMAT book item or DISC digital item and its status was HOLD,
   *     then its status becomes LOAN;
   *   - otherwise its status remains unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public void handleOrder()
  {
    setStatus(OrderStatus.COMPLETED);
    for (ItemLine itemLine : getItemLines())
    {
      LibraryItem libraryItem = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
      if (libraryItem instanceof BookItem)
      {
        BookItem bookItem = (BookItem)libraryItem;
        if (bookItem.getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT && libraryItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD)
        {
          libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.LOAN);
        }
      }
      else if (libraryItem instanceof DigitalItem)
      {
        DigitalItem digitalItem = (DigitalItem)libraryItem;
        if (digitalItem.getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC && libraryItem.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD)
        {
          libraryItem.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.LOAN);
        }
      }
    }
    
  }

```java
   * Only orders with status COMPLETED are considered, and only when this DigitalItem is
   * of type AUDIO and option DOWNLOADABLE.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. If this DigitalItem is not of type AUDIO, return 0.
   * 2. If this DigitalItem's option is not DOWNLOADABLE, return 0.
   * 3. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
   * 4. For each order in orders:
   *    a. If order.status != COMPLETED, skip this order.

   *       i. If itemLine.libraryItem == this, add itemLine.quantity to totalDownloads.

   * - int
```

  public int calculateTotalDownloads(final EList<Order> orders)
  {
    if (getType() != DigitalItemType.AUDIO)
    {
      return 0;
    }

    if (getOption() != DigitalItemOption.DOWNLOADABLE)
    {
      return 0;
    }

    int totalDownloads = 0;
    for (Order order : orders)
    {
      if (order.getStatus() != OrderStatus.COMPLETED)
      {
        continue;

      for (ItemLine itemLine : order.getItemLines())
      {

        }
      }
    }

    return totalDownloads;
  }

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operations
+ DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
+ Order::addItemLine
+ Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
+ Order::handleOrder
+ Order::removeItemLine

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary:
Calculate the total number of downloads for a specific Audio digital item across a list of orders.
Only orders with status COMPLETED are considered, and only when this DigitalItem is DOWNLOADABLE.
Algorithm:
1. If this DigitalItem is not of type AUDIO, return 0.
2. If this DigitalItem.option is DISC, return 0.
3. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
4. For each order in orders:
   a. If order.status != COMPLETED, skip it.
   b. Traverse all itemLines in the order.
   c. For each itemLine, if itemLine.libraryItem is the current DigitalItem, add itemLine.quantity to totalDownloads.
5. Return totalDownloads.
Related Features:
- DigitalItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
- ItemLine.quantity
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, DigitalItemType, DigitalItemOption, OrderStatus
Input:
- orders: a list of Order objects.
- Each Order may contain zero or more ItemLine objects.
- The current object must represent an Audio digital item to be counted.
- Only DOWNLOADABLE items are eligible; DISC items contribute 0.
Output:
- Returns an integer total download count.
- The value is the sum of quantities of matching item lines in all COMPLETED orders.
- Returns 0 if there are no matching downloads.
Pre-condition:
- orders is not null.
- Each order in orders is a valid Order instance.
- Each itemLine.libraryItem reference is valid.
- The operation is invoked on a DigitalItem instance.
Post-condition:
- No system state is modified.
- The returned value equals the total quantity of this Audio DOWNLOADABLE DigitalItem in all COMPLETED orders.

4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::addItemLine
Summary:
Add a new ItemLine to the order, subject to validation rules.
The order must be PENDING, the library item must be AVAILABLE, and duplicate library items are not allowed.
If the item is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem or DISC DigitalItem, its status is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD.
Otherwise, its status remains AVAILABLE.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status != PENDING, return false.
2. If itemLine is null or itemLine.libraryItem is null, return false.
3. If itemLine.libraryItem.status != AVAILABLE, return false.
4. Check existing itemLines in this order:
   a. If any existing itemLine.libraryItem is the same library item as the input itemLine.libraryItem, return false.
5. Append itemLine to order.itemLines.
6. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
7. If item is BookItem and item.type == PRINT_FORMAT, set item.status = HOLD.
8. Else if item is DigitalItem and item.option == DISC, set item.status = HOLD.
9. Otherwise, keep item.status = AVAILABLE.
10. Return true.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- ItemLine.quantity
- LibraryItem.status
- BookItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- itemLine: the ItemLine to be added.
- itemLine.quantity: positive integer quantity for the line.
- itemLine.libraryItem: the associated LibraryItem.
Output:
- Returns true if the item line is successfully added.
- Returns false if any validation fails.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists.
- order.status must be PENDING.
- itemLine and itemLine.libraryItem must not be null.
- itemLine.libraryItem.status must be AVAILABLE.
- No existing item line in the order may reference the same library item.
Post-condition:
- If successful, itemLine is added to order.itemLines.
- If the library item is PRINT_FORMAT BookItem or DISC DigitalItem, its status becomes HOLD.
- Otherwise, the library item remains AVAILABLE.
- If validation fails, neither the order nor the library item status is changed.

Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary:
Count the total quantity of print-format book items in the order, but only if the order is COMPLETED.
If the order is not COMPLETED, return 0.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status != COMPLETED, return 0.
2. Initialize totalCount = 0.
3. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   a. If itemLine.libraryItem is a BookItem and itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
      i. Add itemLine.quantity to totalCount.
4. Return totalCount.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.quantity
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- BookItem.type
Related Classes: ItemLine, BookItem, BookItemType, OrderStatus
Input:
- No explicit input parameters.
- Uses the current Order instance.
Output:
- Returns an integer count of quantities for all print-format book item lines in the order.
- Returns 0 if the order is not COMPLETED or contains no matching items.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists and is valid.
- The current order instance has a defined status and itemLines collection.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The returned value reflects only item lines whose library item is a BookItem with type PRINT_FORMAT in a COMPLETED order.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary:
Remove an existing ItemLine from an order that is still in PENDING status.
If the order is COMPLETED, the operation fails and returns -1.
If removal succeeds, return the updated number of item lines in the order.
If the removed item was previously HOLD (print/disc items), restore its status to AVAILABLE;
otherwise, retain its original status.
Algorithm:
1. If order.status == COMPLETED, return -1.
2. If itemLine is null, return -1.
3. Search order.itemLines for the matching itemLine.
4. If not found, return -1.
5. Remove the matching itemLine from order.itemLines.
6. Let item = removed itemLine.libraryItem.
7. If item.status == HOLD, set item.status = AVAILABLE.
8. Otherwise, keep item.status unchanged.
9. Return order.itemLines.size().
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- LibraryItem.status
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- itemLine: the ItemLine to remove.
Output:
- Returns the updated count of item lines after successful removal.
- Returns -1 if the order is COMPLETED, if itemLine is invalid, or if the item line is not found.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists.
- itemLine is not null.
- The order must not be COMPLETED for removal to proceed.
Post-condition:
- If successful, the specified itemLine is removed from order.itemLines.
- If the removed library item was HOLD, its status becomes AVAILABLE.
- Otherwise, the removed library item's status remains unchanged.
- If failed, the order and library item statuses remain unchanged.

Order::handleOrder
Summary:
Complete the order and update related library item statuses for eligible items.
The order status is set to COMPLETED first.
Then each relevant library item is changed from HOLD to LOAN only when it is a PRINT_FORMAT BookItem or a DISC DigitalItem.
Algorithm:
1. Set order.status = COMPLETED.
2. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   b. If item.status == HOLD:
      i. If item is BookItem and item.type == PRINT_FORMAT, set item.status = LOAN.
      ii. Else if item is DigitalItem and item.option == DISC, set item.status = LOAN.
      iii. Otherwise, keep the status unchanged.
3. Return void.
Related Features:
- Order.status
- Order.itemLines
- ItemLine.libraryItem
- LibraryItem.status
- BookItem.type
- DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input:
- No explicit input parameters.
- Uses the current Order instance.
Output:
- No return value.
- Side effects occur on the order status and eligible library item statuses.
Pre-condition:
- The order exists and contains zero or more item lines.
- Each itemLine.libraryItem reference is valid.
Post-condition:
- order.status becomes COMPLETED.
- Every HOLD print-format BookItem and HOLD disc DigitalItem in the order becomes LOAN.
- All other library items retain their existing status.
